Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = rowIdx + x;\n\n                    const Real center = in[idx];\n                    const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[idx - nx * ny];\n                    const Real top = in[idx + nx * ny];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Copy boundary values: z-faces (full planes)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, y, nz - 1, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, y, nz - 1, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // y-faces and x-faces of the remaining interior slabs\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, ny - 1, z, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, ny - 1, z, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                out[row] = in[row];\n                out[row + nx - 1] = in[row + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":37,"oldLines":7,"newStart":37,"newLines":7,"lines":["     const Real* __restrict in = input.data();","     Real* __restrict out = output.data();"," ","-    #pragma omp parallel","+    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)","     {","         // Process interior points (not on boundaries)","         #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowIdx = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = rowIdx + x;\n\n                    const Real center = in[idx];\n                    const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[idx - nx * ny];\n                    const Real top = in[idx + nx * ny];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Copy boundary values: z-faces (full planes)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t y = 0; Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, \n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict out = output.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowIdx = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = rowIdx + x;\n\n                    const Real center = in[idx];\n                    const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[idx - nx * ny];\n                    const Real top = in[idx + nx * ny];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Copy boundary values: z-faces (full planes)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, y, nz - 1, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, y, nz - 1, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // y-faces and x-faces of the remaining interior slabs\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, ny - 1, z, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, ny - 1, z, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                out[row] = in[row];\n                out[row + nx - 1] = in[row + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Bind threads spread across cores before the OpenMP runtime initializes;\n    // without binding, performance collapses on multi-socket/SMT machines.\n    // Existing user-provided settings are kept (overwrite = 0).\n    setenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\", \"spread\", 0);\n    setenv(\"OMP_PLACES\", \"cores\", 0);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    // Parallel initialization also first-touches pages across threads for NUMA locality\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, \n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict out = output.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowIdx = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = rowIdx + x;\n\n                    const Real center = in[idx];\n                    const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[idx - nx * ny];\n                    const Real top = in[idx + nx * ny];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Copy boundary values: z-faces (full planes)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, y, nz - 1, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, y, nz - 1, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // y-faces and x-faces of the remaining interior slabs\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + idx3(0, ny - 1, z, nx, ny), in + idx3(0, ny - 1, z, nx, ny), nx * sizeof(Real));\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                out[row] = in[row];\n                out[row + nx - 1] = in[row + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Pin each OpenMP thread to a distinct CPU; unbound threads migrate and\n    // performance collapses on multi-socket/SMT machines. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Pin each OpenMP thread to a distinct CPU; unbound threads migrate and\n    // performance collapses on multi-socket/SMT machines. The stencil3d benchmark is now parallelized with OpenMP, bit-exact with the original, and about 4.7x faster out of the box on this 128-core machine (the kernel is memory-bandwidth-bound, so it saturates well below core count).\n\n**Changes to `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp`:**\n- The interior 7-point stencil loop runs under `#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2)` with a `simd` inner loop, rewritten to use precomputed neighbor offsets on `__restrict` pointers for better vectorization.\n- The boundary copy no longer scans the whole grid with a per-cell branch; it copies just the six faces (row `memcpy`s for z/y faces, two scalar writes per row for x faces), also parallelized inside the same parallel region with `nowait` — safe because interior and boundary write disjoint regions and only read the input buffer.\n- Grid initialization is parallelized with the same static schedule, so first-touch places pages on the NUMA node of the thread that later computes them.\n- At startup, unless the user set `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES`, each thread pins itself to a distinct physical core (topology read from sysfs, spread across sockets), and the default thread count is capped at physical cores. This mattered a lot: unpinned, the default 256-thread run was *slower than serial* (806 vs 960 MCUPS) on this dual-socket EPYC; `setenv` in `main` couldn't fix it since libgomp reads env vars before `main`, hence the explicit `sched_setaffinity` approach. SMT siblings are avoided because they add contention, not bandwidth.\n\n**`stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt`** gains `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`; executable name unchanged, no new files or dependencies.\n\n**Correctness:** output hashes are identical to the original serial build across cubic/non-cubic grids, odd/even iteration counts, and tiny grids (e.g. 3³), and results are thread-count-independent (1 vs 256 threads). `-v` validation passes.\n\n**Performance** (384³, 20 iterations; serial original ≈ 960 MCUPS): default run 4.4–4.5 GCUPS, stable across thread counts from 16 up (4.3–4.5 GCUPS at 16/64/128 threads).
